# Solver sources

Links to every solver repository found for each problem, before any solver was analyzed or removed. Problems are in contest order. A repository that covers several problems appears under each of them.

*Collected* repositories were imported into the problem folder. Only Street View Routing, Optimize a Data Center, Loon, Satellites, Pizza, Router Placement, City Plan, Compiling Google, Assembling Smartphones, Software Engineering at Scale, Mentorship and Teamwork and Santa Tracker have been through selection so far, so they are the only problems with repositories marked *not kept*. *Found but not collected* lists duplicates of a collected solver and repositories with no usable solver that the problem's README attributes to that problem. Repositories that were rejected without being attributed to a problem are in [EXCLUDED.md](EXCLUDED.md).

| Problem | Contest | Collected | Found but not collected |
|---|---|---:|---:|
| [Street View Routing](#street-view-routing) | 2014, final | 18 | 0 |
| [Optimize a Data Center](#optimize-a-data-center) | 2015, qualification | 60 | 0 |
| [Loon](#loon) | 2015, final | 29 | 4 |
| [Delivery](#delivery) | 2016, qualification | 182 | 5 |
| [Satellites](#satellites) | 2016, final | 16 | 1 |
| [Pizza](#pizza) | 2017, practice | 362 | 3 |
| [Streaming Videos](#streaming-videos) | 2017, qualification | 381 | 3 |
| [Router Placement](#router-placement) | 2017, final | 25 | 7 |
| [Self-driving Rides](#self-driving-rides) | 2018, qualification | 544 | 7 |
| [City Plan](#city-plan) | 2018, final | 14 | 3 |
| [Photo Slideshow](#photo-slideshow) | 2019, qualification | 557 | 8 |
| [Compiling Google](#compiling-google) | 2019, final | 12 | 2 |
| [More Pizza](#more-pizza) | 2020, practice | 301 | 2 |
| [Book Scanning](#book-scanning) | 2020, qualification | 749 | 15 |

### Found but not collected (4)

- [TomDaniaud/Polyhash](https://github.com/TomDaniaud/Polyhash) — an older snapshot of the same team's project. `GabrielPlayeur/polyhash` has every one of its commits plus three newer ones.
- [AlexysGromard/polyhash](https://github.com/AlexysGromard/polyhash) — only the course template with the team's names added. `IIXIVII/polyhash2024` is the same team's repo and contains this commit and the full solver.
- `polyhash2024_starter` (no URL recorded) — the unmodified Poly# 2024 starter template (stub parser and solver). The full repos above build on it.
- [TomDaniaud/Polyhash](https://github.com/TomDaniaud/Polyhash) — older snapshot of the same team's repo (TomDaniaud/Polyhash at b135878; every commit is in GabrielPlayeur/polyhash) as `polyhash_GabrielPlayeur`.

### Found but not collected (5)

- [Valalol/polyhash2023](https://github.com/Valalol/polyhash2023) — byte-identical to `polyhash2023_E218577J`. It is the same project mirrored to GitHub, so it is recorded as a mirror above instead of being copied again.
- [loneycoffee/polyhashcode2023](https://github.com/loneycoffee/polyhashcode2023) — a snapshot of `JustAlternate/polyhash2023-googlehashcodeFinale` with the authors' names shortened and the `solutions/` placeholders removed.
- [dmadjib/Polyhash_2023](https://github.com/dmadjib/Polyhash_2023) — a re-upload of `lina-el-arajna/polyhash` by one of its co-authors. It has an English README and renamed entry files, and the solver code differs only in comments and whitespace. The original (with author headers and CI) is kept.
- `polyhash2023-main` and `polyhash2023-main (1)` (no URL recorded) — identical copies of the unmodified Poly# 2023 starter template.
- `polyhash-bootstrap-main` (no URL recorded) — course bootstrap template with a Delivery-format parser stub and no solver. Apart from a few comment lines it matches the 2023 template.
